# PDF request list — all substances

**2233 papers for you to save.** The evidence maps list 3344 unique papers (43 maps; 258 duplicates collapsed). Of these, 21 you have already saved, and 1090 are free in PubMed Central or at the publisher, so I read those myself (see `docs/READ-MYSELF-LIST.md`). Of the papers below, 553 have not yet been checked for a free full text (that check is running; every one found free moves to my own reading list, so the number for you will fall); the rest are paywalled or on ScienceDirect, which refuses the app's browser; 30 of them are marked **free, open access**: free to download, but I cannot open them. Only items 1–10 are needed now.

**How to work through this:** in order, ten at a time. For each paper: open the link, save the PDF into `DrugEncyclopedia/PDFs/<folder>/` with the exact filename shown (if your save dialog adds `.pdf` by itself, type the name without `.pdf`). Tell me when a batch is done and I start reading. Tier A of every substance comes first because those papers turn draft claims into verified ones.

Per-substance detail (journal, year, tier, search log) is in `PDFs/<Substance>/_DOI-LIST.md`.
